#!/usr/bin/env python3
import re

docs = {
    'SYSTEM_GUIDE': '/home/jacobw/quantstack/docs/SYSTEM_GUIDE.md',
    'docs/README': '/home/jacobw/quantstack/docs/README.md',
    'INDEX': '/home/jacobw/quantstack/docs/INDEX.md',
    'root/README': '/home/jacobw/quantstack/README.md',
}

def extract_section(file, start_pattern, end_pattern=None):
    try:
        with open(file) as f:
            content = f.read()
            match = re.search(f'{start_pattern}.*?(?={end_pattern}|$)', content, re.DOTALL)
            return match.group(0)[:500] if match else None
    except:
        return None

# Check Architecture sections
print("=== ARCHITECTURE SECTIONS ===")
for name, path in docs.items():
    arch = extract_section(path, r'^## Architecture', r'^##')
    if arch:
        print(f"\n{name}: {len(arch)} chars")
        print(arch[:200] + "...")

# Check Operations sections  
print("\n\n=== OPERATIONS/COMMANDS SECTIONS ===")
for name, path in docs.items():
    ops = extract_section(path, r'(## Operations|## Quick Commands)', r'^##')
    if ops:
        print(f"\n{name}: {len(ops)} chars")
        print(ops[:200] + "...")
EOF

+## Entry Conditions

-**SHORT Entry**:
-- Price >= VWAP * 1.005 (0.5% above VWAP)
-- L2 Ratio <= 0.858 (ask depth > bid depth)
-- Time: 09:35-15:30 ET

-### Exit Conditions
-
-**Take Profit**:
-- LONG: +0.5% from entry
-- SHORT: -0.5% from entry
-
-**Stop Loss**:
-- LONG: -0.75% from entry
-- SHORT: +0.75% from entry

+- **Short**: `close >= VWAP * 1.005` AND `l2_ratio <= 0.858`
+- Entry window: 09:35-15:30 ET
+- One position at a time
+
+## Exit Conditions (Bracket Orders)
+
+- **Take Profit**: +0.5% (long), -0.5% (short)
+- **Stop Loss**: -0.75% (long), +0.75% (short)
+- **Mean Reversion**: Price crosses VWAP
+- **Forced Exit**: 15:55 ET (EOD flatten)

[38;5;141mcat > /tmp/check_package_docs.sh << 'EOF'
#!/bin/bash

echo "=== QX PACKAGES DOCUMENTATION STATUS ==="
for dir in /home/jacobw/quantstack/qx-*; do
  pkg=$(basename "$dir")
  
  # Check for README
  readme=$(find "$dir" -maxdepth 1 -name "README.md" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  
  # Check for docs directory
  docs_dir=$(find "$dir" -maxdepth 1 -type d -name "docs" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  
  # Check for docstrings in main module
  main_py=$(find "$dir" -name "__init__.py" -path "*/src/*" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  has_docstring=""
  if [ -n "$main_py" ]; then
    if grep -q '"""' "$main_py" 2>/dev/null; then
      has_docstring="✓"
    else
      has_docstring="✗"
    fi
  fi
  
  # Check pyproject.toml for description
  pyproject="$dir/pyproject.toml"
  description=""
  if [ -f "$pyproject" ]; then
    description=$(grep "^description" "$pyproject" 2>/dev/null | cut -d'"' -f2)
  fi
  
  echo ""
  echo "📦 $pkg"
  [ -n "$readme" ] && echo "  README: ✅ $(wc -l < "$readme") lines" || echo "  README: ❌ Missing"
  [ -n "$docs_dir" ] && echo "  docs/: ✅ $(ls "$docs_dir" 2>/dev/null | wc -l) files" || echo "  docs/: ❌ Missing"
  [ -n "$has_docstring" ] && echo "  Docstrings: $has_docstring" || echo "  Docstrings: ? (no __init__.py)"
  [ -n "$description" ] && echo "  Description: $description" || echo "  Description: ❌ Missing"
done

echo ""
echo ""
echo "=== TRADING SYSTEMS DOCUMENTATION ==="
for dir in /home/jacobw/quantstack/l2_scalping /home/jacobw/quantstack/l2_vwap_reversion; do
  sys=$(basename "$dir")
  
  readme="$dir/README.md"
  docs_dir="$dir/docs"
  
  echo ""
  echo "🎯 $sys"
  [ -f "$readme" ] && echo "  README: ✅ $(wc -l < "$readme") lines" || echo "  README: ❌ Missing"
  [ -d "$docs_dir" ] && echo "  docs/: ✅ $(ls "$docs_dir" 2>/dev/null | wc -l) files" || echo "  docs/: ❌ Missing"
  
  # List docs if they exist
  if [ -d "$docs_dir" ]; then
    ls "$docs_dir"/*.md 2>/dev/null | while read doc; do
      echo "    - $(basename "$doc")"
    done
  fi
done
EOF
chmod +x /tmp/check_package_docs.sh
/tmp/check_package_docs.sh

#!/bin/bash

echo "=== PUBLIC API DOCUMENTATION ==="

# Check qx-core (most important - contracts and types)
echo ""
echo "📦 qx-core (Core contracts and types)"
find /home/jacobw/quantstack/qx-core/src -name "*.py" -type f 2>/dev/null | while read f; do
  classes=$(grep -c "^class " "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
  funcs=$(grep -c "^def " "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
  docstrings=$(grep -c '"""' "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
  if [ $classes -gt 0 ] || [ $funcs -gt 0 ]; then
    rel=$(echo "$f" | sed 's|.*/qx-core/src/||')
    echo "  $rel: $classes classes, $funcs functions, $docstrings docstrings"
  fi
done | head -10

# Check qx-broker (IBKR integration)
echo ""
echo "📦 qx-broker (IBKR integration)"
find /home/jacobw/quantstack/qx-broker/src -name "*.py" -type f 2>/dev/null | while read f; do
  classes=$(grep -c "^class " "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
  if [ $classes -gt 0 ]; then
    rel=$(echo "$f" | sed 's|.*/qx-broker/src/||')
    echo "  $rel: $classes classes"
  fi
done | head -5

# Check qx-risk (risk management)
echo ""
echo "📦 qx-risk (Risk management)"
find /home/jacobw/quantstack/qx-risk/src -name "*.py" -type f 2>/dev/null | while read f; do
  classes=$(grep -c "^class " "$f" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
  if [ $classes -gt 0 ]; then
    rel=$(echo "$f" | sed 's|.*/qx-risk/src/||')
    echo "  $rel: $classes classes"
  fi
done | head -5

# Check for missing __init__.py docstrings
echo ""
echo "=== MISSING PACKAGE DOCSTRINGS ==="
for pkg in qx-core qx-broker qx-data qx-features qx-risk; do
  init="/home/jacobw/quantstack/$pkg/src/${pkg//-/_}/__init__.py"
  if [ -f "$init" ]; then
    if ! grep -q '"""' "$init" 2>/dev/null; then
      echo "❌ $pkg/__init__.py - No module docstring"
    fi
  fi
done
EOF
chmod +x /tmp/check_api_docs.sh

[0m[38;5;10m# QuantStack Documentation Style Guide

## Database
- Primary: "PostgreSQL database `trading`"
- Informal: "trade database"
- Code: `trade_journal` (keep as-is)

## Service Names
- Prose: "L2 Scalping", "Intraday Paper"
- Systemd: `l2-scalping.service`
- Code: `l2_scalping/`

## Exit Reasons
- Code: `STOP_LOSS`, `TAKE_PROFIT`, `MAX_HOLD_TIME`
- Prose: "stop-loss", "take-profit"

## Time
- Always: "HH:MM ET"
- Ranges: "09:26-17:01 ET"

## Paths
- Docs: `~/quantstack/...`
- Scripts: `/home/jacobw/quantstack/...`

## Acronyms
- Prose: "VWAP", "L2", "SIP" (uppercase)
- Code: `vwap`, `l2_`, `sip_` (lowercase)

[0m[38;5;10m# Configuration Guide

## strategy.yaml
- entry.obi_threshold: Order book imbalance threshold (0.0-1.0)
  - Default: 0.8
  - Higher = more selective entries
  - Validated in backtests: 0.8 optimal for 5-min holds

## risk.yaml
- max_position_size: Maximum shares per position
- daily_loss_limit: Stop trading after this loss
- stop_loss_bps: Stop-loss in basis points (default: 10)

## ibkr.yaml
- order_client_id_base: Starting client ID (200-299 range)
- data_client_id_base: Data connection client ID (250)

[0m[38;5;10m# Troubleshooting

## No trades being placed
- Check: SIP universe generated today
- Check: Market hours (09:26-17:01 ET)
- Check: Signals being generated (logs)

## Bracket orders rejected (Error 110)
- Cause: Invalid price precision
- Fix: Prices rounded to tick size (0.01)

## Zombie depth subscriptions (Error 309)
- Cause: Max 3 symbols per account
- Fix: Run clear_ibkr_depth_subscriptions.py

[0m[38;5;10m# Configuration Guide

## strategy.yaml
- vwap_threshold_long: 0.995 (enter when price <= VWAP * 0.995)
- vwap_threshold_short: 1.005 (enter when price >= VWAP * 1.005)
- l2_ratio_long: 1.165 (bid depth must exceed ask depth)
- l2_ratio_short: 0.858 (ask depth must exceed bid depth)
- stop_loss_pct: 0.75% (stop-loss distance)
- take_profit_pct: 0.50% (take-profit distance)

## ibkr.yaml
- order_client_id: 300 (IBKR client ID for orders)
- data_client_id: 350 (IBKR client ID for data)

[0m[38;5;10m# System Architecture

## Components
- main.py: Main trading loop and EOD handling
- strategy.py: VWAP mean reversion logic
- vwap.py: VWAP calculator
- l2_filter.py: L2 depth ratio filter
- data/bar_feed.py: 1-minute bar feed from IBKR
- data/l2_reader.py: L2 data from l2-scalping output
- execution/order_manager.py: Bracket order management

## Data Flow
SIP → l2-scalping → L2 parquet files → l2_reader → strategy → order_manager → IBKR

[1mmarkdown
[0m[38;5;10m# Risk Controls

## Position Limits
- One position at a time
- Entry window: 09:35-15:30 ET

## Exit Controls
- Bracket orders (stop-loss + take-profit)
- Mean reversion exit (price crosses VWAP)
- Forced EOD exit at 15:55 ET

## Dependencies
- Requires l2-scalping running (for L2 data)
- Shares 3-symbol limit with l2-scalping

[0m[38;5;10m# Troubleshooting

## No trades being placed
- Check: l2-scalping is running (provides L2 data)
- Check: L2 data files exist in ~/quantstack/data/l2_maximum/features/
- Check: VWAP threshold met (price deviation from VWAP)
- Check: L2 ratio threshold met (bid/ask depth)

## Bracket orders rejected
- Check: Tick size rounding (0.01)
- Check: Stop-loss/take-profit prices valid

## No L2 data
- Cause: l2-scalping not running
- Fix: Start l2-scalping service first

Thinking...[2K[1G[0m[0mI will run the following command: [38;5;141mcat > /tmp/qx_packages_audit.sh << 'EOF'
#!/bin/bash

echo "📦 QX-* PACKAGES DOCUMENTATION AUDIT"
echo ""

for dir in /home/jacobw/quantstack/qx-*; do
  pkg=$(basename "$dir")
  
  # Check README
  readme=""
  readme_lines=0
  if [ -f "$dir/README.md" ]; then
    readme="✅"
    readme_lines=$(wc -l < "$dir/README.md")
  else
    readme="❌"
  fi
  
  # Check docs directory
  docs_dir=""
  docs_count=0
  if [ -d "$dir/docs" ]; then
    docs_dir="✅"
    docs_count=$(find "$dir/docs" -name "*.md" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
  else
    docs_dir="❌"
  fi
  
  # Check pyproject.toml description
  description=""
  if [ -f "$dir/pyproject.toml" ]; then
    description=$(grep "^description" "$dir/pyproject.toml" 2>/dev/null | cut -d'"' -f2)
  fi
  
  # Count Python files
  py_files=$(find "$dir/src" -name "*.py" -type f ! -path "*__pycache__*" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
  
  # Check for module docstring
  init_file=$(find "$dir/src" -name "__init__.py" -path "*/${pkg//-/_}/__init__.py" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  module_doc="?"
  if [ -n "$init_file" ] && [ -f "$init_file" ]; then
    if grep -q '"""' "$init_file" 2>/dev/null; then
      module_doc="✅"
    else
      module_doc="❌"
    fi
  fi
  
  echo "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
  echo "📦 $pkg"
  echo "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
  echo "  README:          $readme $([ $readme_lines -gt 0 ] && echo "($readme_lines lines)" || echo "")"
  echo "  docs/:           $docs_dir $([ $docs_count -gt 0 ] && echo "($docs_count files)" || echo "")"
  echo "  Module docstring: $module_doc"
  echo "  Python files:    $py_files"
  [ -n "$description" ] && echo "  Description:     $description"
  echo ""
done

echo ""
echo "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
echo "SUMMARY"
echo "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
echo ""
total=$(ls -d /home/jacobw/quantstack/qx-* 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
with_readme=$(find /home/jacobw/quantstack/qx-* -maxdepth 1 -name "README.md" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
with_docs=$(find /home/jacobw/quantstack/qx-* -maxdepth 1 -type d -name "docs" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
echo "Total packages:        $total"
echo "With README:           $with_readme / $total"
echo "With docs/:            $with_docs / $total"
echo "Missing README:        $((total - with_readme)) packages"
echo "Missing docs/:         $((total - with_docs)) packages"
EOF
chmod +x /tmp/qx_packages_audit.sh
/tmp/qx_packages_audit.sh

[0m[38;5;10m# qx-core

Core contracts, types, config validators, and state management for QuantStack.

## Schemas

Located in `src/qx_core/schemas.py`:
- `Bar` - OHLCV bar data
- `Signal` - Trading signals
- `Trade` - Trade records
- `Position` - Position tracking
- `Order` - Order data
- `Fill` - Fill executions

## Contracts

Located in `src/qx_core/contracts.py`:
- `DataContract` - Data provider interface
- `StrategyContract` - Strategy interface
- `BrokerContract` - Broker interface

## Validators

Located in `src/qx_core/validators.py`:
- Config validation
- Schema validation
- Type checking

## Regime Detection

Located in `src/qx_core/regime/`:
- `detector.py` - HMM-based regime detection
- `monitoring.py` - Regime monitoring

## Usage

[0mpython[0m[0m
from qx_core.schemas import Bar, Signal[0m[0m
from qx_core.contracts import StrategyContract[0m[0m
[0m[0m
[38;5;252m[1m# Use schemas[0m[0m
bar = Bar(symbol="AAPL", open=150.0, high=151.0, low=149.0, close=150.5, volume=1000000)[0m[0m
[0m[0m
[38;5;252m[1m# Implement contracts[0m[0m
class MyStrategy(StrategyContract):[0m[0m
   def generate_signals(self, bars):[0m[0m
       # Your logic here[0m[0m
       pass[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
## Installation

[0mbash[0m[0m
pip install -e .[0m[0m
[38;5;10m[0m[0m[0m

[1mmarkdown
[0m[38;5;10m# qx-broker

IBKR broker integration with gateway control, health monitoring, and connection management.

## Components

### Gateway Control
Located in `src/qx_broker/gateway/control.py`:
- Start/stop IBKR Gateway
- Connection lifecycle management

### Health Monitoring
Located in `src/qx_broker/gateway/health.py`:
- Connection health checks
- Error detection
- Auto-recovery

### Client ID Management
- Automatic client ID allocation
- Prevents ID conflicts
- Handles reconnections

## Client ID Allocation

| Service | Client IDs | Purpose |
|---------|------------|---------|
| intraday-paper | 100-199 | Paper trading |
| l2-scalping | 200-299 | L2 scalping (orders + data) |
| l2-vwap | 300-399 | VWAP reversion (orders + data) |
| position-monitor | 998 | Read-only monitoring |

## Usage

[0mpython[0m[0m
from qx_broker.gateway import GatewayControl, HealthMonitor[0m[0m
[0m[0m
[38;5;252m[1m# Start gateway[0m[0m
gateway = GatewayControl()[0m[0m
gateway.start()[0m[0m
[0m[0m
[38;5;252m[1m# Monitor health[0m[0m
health = HealthMonitor()[0m[0m
status = health.check()[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
## Configuration

Connection settings in trading system configs:
- Host: 127.0.0.1
- Port: 7494 (IBKR Gateway)
- Client ID: Allocated per service

## Error Handling

Common errors:
- Error 309: Max depth subscriptions (3 per account)
- Error 326: Client ID conflict
- Connection timeout: Gateway not running

See `/home/jacobw/quantstack/docs/IBKR_IB_INSYNC_CONNECTION_PROTOCOL.md` for details.

[0m[38;5;10m# qx-risk

Risk management policies including stop-loss rules and position sizing.

## Stop-Loss Policies

### ATR-Based Stops
Located in `src/qx_risk/atr_stop.py`:
- Dynamic stops based on Average True Range
- Adapts to volatility

## Usage

[0mpython[0m[0m
from qx_risk import ATRStop[0m[0m
[0m[0m
[38;5;252m[1m# Create ATR-based stop[0m[0m
stop = ATRStop(atr_multiplier=2.0)[0m[0m
stop_price = stop.calculate(entry_price=100.0, atr=1.5, direction="long")[0m[0m
[38;5;252m[1m# Returns: 97.0 (100 - 2*1.5)[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
## Position Sizing

Risk-based position sizing:
- Fixed dollar risk per trade
- Percentage of portfolio
- Kelly criterion

## Risk Limits

- Daily loss limits
- Maximum position size
- Concentration limits

## Installation

[0mbash[0m[0m
pip install -e .[0m[0m

## Files

- `config/strategy.yaml` - Trading strategy parameters
- `config/risk.yaml` - Risk management settings
- `config/ibkr.yaml` - IBKR connection settings

## strategy.yaml

### Entry Parameters
[0myaml[0m[0m
entry:[0m[0m
 obi_threshold: 0.8  # Order book imbalance threshold (0.0-1.0)[0m[0m
                     # Higher = more selective entries[0m[0m
                     # Default: 0.8 (validated in backtests)[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
### Timing Parameters
[0myaml[0m[0m
timing:[0m[0m
 default_hold_seconds: 300  # Target hold time (5 minutes)[0m[0m
 max_hold_seconds: 600      # Force exit after 10 minutes[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
### Time-of-Day Filter
[0myaml[0m[0m
tod_filter: "closing"  # Only trade during closing (15:30-16:00 ET)[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
## risk.yaml

### Position Limits
[0myaml[0m[0m
max_position_size: 100      # Maximum shares per position[0m[0m
daily_loss_limit: 500.00    # Stop trading after $500 loss[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
### Stop-Loss
[0myaml[0m[0m
stop_loss_bps: 10  # Stop-loss at 10 basis points (0.1%)[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
## ibkr.yaml

### Client IDs
[0myaml[0m[0m
order_client_id_base: 200  # Orders: 200-299 range[0m[0m
data_client_id_base: 250   # Data: 250-299 range[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
### Connection
[0myaml[0m[0m
host: "127.0.0.1"[0m[0m
port: 7494  # IBKR Gateway port[0m[0m
timeout: 30[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
## Examples

### Conservative Settings
[0myaml[0m[0m
[38;5;252m[1m# strategy.yaml[0m[0m
entry:[0m[0m

[0m[0m
[38;5;252m[1m# risk.yaml[0m[0m

[0m[0m

[0m[38;5;10m# L2 Scalping Troubleshooting

## No Trades Being Placed

**Symptoms:** Service running but no trades executed

**Check:**
1. SIP universe generated today
   [0mbash[0m[0m
  ls ~/intraday_stack/data/daily_sip/date=$(date +%Y-%m-%d)/[0m[0m
  [38;5;10m[0m[38;5;10m
2. Market hours (09:26-17:01 ET)
3. Signals being generated
   [0m[38;5;10m[0mbash[0m[0m
  journalctl -u l2-scalping -n 100 | grep "Signal"[0m[0m
  [38;5;10m[0m[38;5;10m

**Common causes:**
- No SIP universe → Run [0mgenerate_daily_sip_universe.py[38;5;10m
- Outside market hours → Wait for market open
- No signals meeting threshold → Check [0mstrategy.yaml[38;5;10m thresholds

---

## Bracket Orders Rejected (Error 110)

**Symptoms:** Orders rejected with "Invalid price"

**Cause:** Price precision not rounded to tick size (0.01)

**Fix:** Already implemented in code ([0mround_to_tick_size()[38;5;10m)

**Verify:**
[0m[38;5;10m[0mbash[0m[0m
journalctl -u l2-scalping | grep "Error 110"[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
---

## Zombie Depth Subscriptions (Error 309)

**Symptoms:** "Max depth subscriptions exceeded"

**Cause:** IBKR limit of 3 symbols per account

**Fix:**
[0mbash[0m[0m
python3 ~/quantstack/scripts/clear_ibkr_depth_subscriptions.py[0m[0m
sudo systemctl restart l2-scalping[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
**Prevention:** L2 health monitor auto-recovers

---

## Position Held Overnight

**Symptoms:** Position not closed by EOD

**Check safeguards:**
1. Bracket orders placed?
   [0mbash[0m[0m
  journalctl -u l2-scalping | grep "Bracket order placed"[0m[0m
  [38;5;10m[0m[38;5;10m
2. Force exit triggered?
   [0m[38;5;10m[0mbash[0m[0m
  journalctl -u l2-scalping | grep "FORCE EXIT"[0m[0m
  [38;5;10m[0m[38;5;10m
3. Emergency EOD ran?
   [0m[38;5;10m[0mbash[0m[0m
  journalctl -u emergency-eod-close[0m[0m
  [38;5;10m[0m[38;5;10m

**Verify config:**
[0m[38;5;10m[0myaml[0m[0m
[38;5;252m[1m# strategy.yaml[0m[0m
timing:[0m[0m
 max_hold_seconds: 600  # Should be 600[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
---

## No L2 Data Being Written

**Symptoms:** No parquet files in data directory

**Check:**
[0mbash[0m[0m
find ~/quantstack/data/l2_maximum/features/date=$(date +%Y-%m-%d) -name "*.parquet" -newermt "5 min ago"[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
**Common causes:**
- Service not running → `systemctl status l2-scalping`
- No symbols subscribed → Check SIP universe
- IBKR connection lost → Check Gateway

---

## Database Connection Errors

**Symptoms:** "Could not connect to PostgreSQL"

**Check:**
[0mbash[0m[0m
psql -d trading -U jacobw -c "SELECT 1"[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
**Fix:** Ensure PostgreSQL is running
[0mbash[0m[0m
sudo systemctl status postgresql[0m[0m
[38;5;10m[0m[0m[0m

[0m[38;5;10m# L2 VWAP Reversion Configuration Guide

## Files

- `config/strategy.yaml` - Strategy parameters
- `config/ibkr.yaml` - IBKR connection settings

## strategy.yaml

### VWAP Thresholds
[0myaml[0m[0m
vwap_threshold_long: 0.995   # Enter long when price <= VWAP [3m 0.995 (0.5% below)[0m[0m
vwap[23mthreshold_short: 1.005  # Enter short when price >= VWAP [3m 1.005 (0.5% above)[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
### L2 Filter Thresholds
[0myaml[0m[0m
l2[23mratio_long: 1.165   # Bid depth must be 16.5% higher than ask depth[0m[0m
l2_ratio_short: 0.858  # Ask depth must be 14.2% higher than bid depth[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
### Exit Parameters
[0myaml[0m[0m
stop_loss_pct: 0.75    # Stop-loss at 0.75% from entry[0m[0m
take_profit_pct: 0.50  # Take-profit at 0.50% from entry[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
### Trading Window
[0myaml[0m[0m
entry_start: "09:35"  # Start entries at 09:35 ET[0m[0m
entry_end: "15:30"    # Stop entries at 15:30 ET[0m[0m
eod_flatten: "15:55"  # Force close at 15:55 ET[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
## ibkr.yaml

### Client IDs
[0myaml[0m[0m
order_client_id: 300  # Orders[0m[0m
data_client_id: 350   # Data (bars)[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
### Connection
[0myaml[0m[0m
host: "127.0.0.1"[0m[0m
port: 7494[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
## Research Basis

Strategy parameters based on research:
- Source: `/home/jacobw/quantresearch/projects/l2_vwap_spy_corr/`
- Variant: `spread_off`
- Expectancy: 15.32
- Win rate: 67.5%

## Tuning Guidelines

### More Conservative
[0myaml[0m[0m
vwap_threshold_long: 0.990   # Wider deviation (1.0%)[0m[0m
l2_ratio_long: 1.200         # Stronger L2 signal[0m[0m
stop_loss_pct: 0.50          # Tighter stop[0m[0m
[38;5;10m
### More Aggressive
[0myaml[0m[0m
vwap_threshold_long: 0.998   # Narrower deviation (0.2%)[0m[0m
l2_ratio_long: 1.100         # Weaker L2 signal[0m[0m
stop_loss_pct: 1.00          # Wider stop[0m[0m
